seven tenths of a number is 91. what is the number? 91 is seven tenths of 130: 91 ÷ 7 = 13, 13 × 10 = 130.
four tenths of 70 is 28: 70 ÷ 10 = 7, 7 × 4 = 28.
what is two fifths of 115? two fifths of 115 is 46: 115 ÷ 5 = 23, 23 × 2 = 46.
six sevenths of 98 is 84: 98 ÷ 7 = 14, 14 × 6 = 84.
four ninths of the students have a pen; 80 do not. how many students are there? 80 is five ninths of 144: 9 − 4 = 5, 80 ÷ 5 = 16, 16 × 9 = 144. there are 144 students in all.
